  SponsoredRunCredential,
} from '@codebuff/common/ads/sponsored-run-credentials'
import type { SponsoredSignupStep } from '@codebuff/common/ads/sponsored-signup-step'

export const SPONSORED_SIGNUP_TOOL_NAME = 'request_sponsored_signup'

/**
 * How long before the run's local cutoff the account step gives up, as
 * Desktop's `SPONSORED_CONNECT_GRANT_MARGIN_MS`: the question has no timer of
 * its own, so it waits while the grant can still fund the rest of the run.
 */
export const SPONSORED_ACCOUNT_STEP_MARGIN_MS = 5 * 60_000

/** MIRRORED: Desktop's answers to the sign-in question. */
export const SPONSORED_SIGNUP_YES = 'Yes, I’m signed in'
export const SPONSORED_SIGNUP_NO = 'No, I don’t want to sign up'

/** What the chat shows while the run waits on the user. Never a value. */
export type SponsoredAccountAsk =
  | {
      kind: 'signin'
      offerName: string
      url: string
      /** False when no browser could be opened here: the link is the way in. */
      opened: boolean
    }
  | {
      kind: 'credentials'
      offerName: string
      credentials: readonly SponsoredRunCredential[]
      /** Why the last submission was refused, to show under the field. */
      error: string | null
    }

export type SponsoredAccountAnswer =
  | { kind: 'signin'; confirmed: boolean }
  | { kind: 'credentials'; values: Readonly<Record<string, string>> }
  | { kind: 'skip' }

export type SponsoredAccountResult = {
  status: 'confirmed' | 'declined' | 'unavailable'
  note: string
}

/**
 * A run's connected credentials. A class with a PRIVATE field, as Desktop's
 * `SponsoredRunCredentialSet`: spread, `Object.entries`, `JSON.stringify` and
 * `util.inspect` see nothing, so a stray log line cannot carry a value.
 */
export class SponsoredHeldCredentials {
  #secrets: SponsoredRedactableSecret[] = []

  hold(secrets: readonly SponsoredRedactableSecret[]): void {
    this.#secrets = secrets.map((secret) => ({ ...secret }))
  }

  forget(): void {
    this.#secrets = []
  }

  /** The declared names held. Names are not secret. */
  get names(): string[] {
    return this.#secrets.map((secret) => secret.name)
  }

  get size(): number {
    return this.#secrets.length
  }

  /** What the sandbox broker injects, after its scrub. A fresh object. */
  env(): Record<string, string> | undefined {
    if (this.#secrets.length === 0) return undefined
    const env: Record<string, string> = {}
    for (const secret of this.#secrets) env[secret.name] = secret.value
    return env
  }

  redact(text: string): string {
    return this.#secrets.length === 0
      ? text
      : redactSponsoredCredentialText(text, this.#secrets)
  }

  redactDeep<T>(value: T): T {
    return this.#secrets.length === 0
      ? value
      : redactSponsoredCredentialDeep(value, this.#secrets)
  }

  toJSON(): string {
    return '[redacted]'
  }

  toString(): string {
    return '[redacted]'
  }

  [inspect.custom](): string {
    return `SponsoredHeldCredentials(${this.names.join(', ') || 'empty'}) [redacted]`
  }
}

/** User copy for a refused key. Never names a value. */
export function sponsoredCredentialRefusalCopy(
  credential: SponsoredRunCredential,
  problem: string,
): string {
  if (problem === 'empty') return `Enter your ${credential.label}, or skip.`
  if (problem === 'too_short')
    return `That ${credential.label} looks too short. Paste the whole key.`
  if (problem === 'too_long')
    return `That ${credential.label} is too long. Paste the key on its own.`
  return `That ${credential.label} has spaces or line breaks in it. Paste the key on its own.`
}

/**
 * MIRRORED from Desktop's `sponsoredCredentialSection` `savedTo` lines: where
 * the key went for the app to read after the run.
 */
export function sponsoredCredentialSavedLine(
  count: number,
  savedTo: string | undefined,
): string {
  return savedTo
    ? `[freebuff product prompt text omitted]${count === 1 ? 'it' : 'them'}[freebuff product prompt text omitted]${savedTo}[freebuff product prompt text omitted]${count === 1 ? '' : 's'}[freebuff product prompt text omitted]${count === 1 ? 'it' : 'them'}[freebuff product prompt text omitted]${savedTo}[freebuff product prompt text omitted]`
    : '- Freebuff did NOT save the value to an env file in this project, and this run cannot write one. In your final message, tell the user to add it to their local env file themselves, naming the variable.'
}

export type SponsoredAccountStepDeps = {
  offerName: string
  /** The tracked signup link (`sponsoredSignupStep`), or none. */
  signup: SponsoredSignupStep | undefined
  /** What the procedure declares (`declaredRunCredentials`). */
  credentials: readonly SponsoredRunCredential[]
  held: SponsoredHeldCredentials
  /** Opens a link in the user's browser; false when none could be opened. */
  openUrl: (url: string) => Promise<boolean>
  /** Shows (or, with null, clears) what the chat asks the user. */
  show: (ask: SponsoredAccountAsk | null) => void
  /** Saves accepted keys to the project's env file; the file name, if saved. */
  save: (
    values: Readonly<Record<string, string>>,
  ) => Promise<string | undefined>
  /** When the step stops waiting and continues without the user. */
  deadlineMs: () => number
  now: () => number
}

type Waiting = {
  ask: SponsoredAccountAsk
  answer: (answer: SponsoredAccountAnswer | 'timeout') => void
}

/**
 * One run's account step. `request` is what the tool calls; `answer` is what
 * the chat calls when the user responds.
 */
export class SponsoredAccountStep {
  private result: SponsoredAccountResult | null = null
  private pending: Promise<SponsoredAccountResult> | null = null
  private waiting: Waiting | null = null
  private confirmed = false
  private disposed = false

  constructor(private readonly deps: SponsoredAccountStepDeps) {}

  /** The user said Yes to the sign-in: a skill's `user_confirmed` passes. */
  get signupConfirmed(): boolean {
    return this.confirmed
  }

  /** Whether the run is waiting on the user right now. */
  get asking(): SponsoredAccountAsk | null {
    return this.waiting?.ask ?? null
  }

  /** The run ended: anything waiting continues as declined. */
  dispose(): void {
    this.disposed = true
    this.waiting?.answer({ kind: 'skip' })
  }

  request(signal: AbortSignal): Promise<SponsoredAccountResult> {
    if (signal.aborted || this.disposed)
      return Promise.resolve({
        status: 'unavailable',
        note: LAB_SIGNUP_STOPPED_NOTE,
      })
    if (this.result) return Promise.resolve(this.result)
    if (this.pending) return this.pending
    this.pending = this.run(signal)
      .then((result) => {
        if (result.status !== 'unavailable') this.result = result
        return result
      })
      .finally(() => {
        this.pending = null
      })
    return this.pending
  }

  /**
   * The user's response. A refused key leaves the form up with the reason and
   * returns it; everything else resolves the step and returns null.
   */
  answer(answer: SponsoredAccountAnswer): string | null {
    const waiting = this.waiting
    if (!waiting) return null
    if (answer.kind === 'credentials' && waiting.ask.kind === 'credentials') {
      for (const credential of waiting.ask.credentials) {
        const value = answer.values[credential.env]?.trim() ?? ''
        const problem = sponsoredCredentialValueProblem(value)
        if (problem) {
          const error = sponsoredCredentialRefusalCopy(credential, problem)
          waiting.ask = { ...waiting.ask, error }
          this.deps.show(waiting.ask)
          return error
        }
      }
    }
    waiting.answer(answer)
    return null
  }

  private async run(signal: AbortSignal): Promise<SponsoredAccountResult> {
    const { signup, credentials } = this.deps
    if (!signup) {
      if (credentials.length === 0)
        return { status: 'unavailable', note: LAB_SIGNUP_NO_STEP_NOTE }
      const connected = await this.connect(signal)
      if (!connected) return this.declined()
      return { status: 'confirmed', note: labConnectedNote(connected) }
    }
    // The `{email}` slot, if any, is removed: no prefill without consent.
    const url = signup.url ? signupUrlWithEmail(signup.url, null) : null
    if (!url) {
      return {
        status: 'unavailable',
        note: 'Tracked signup is unavailable. Do not open an untracked link. Report the remaining account-dependent steps as blocked.',
      }
    }
    const opened = await this.deps.openUrl(url).catch(() => false)
    const answer = await this.wait(
      { kind: 'signin', offerName: this.deps.offerName, url, opened },
      signal,
    )
    if (answer.kind !== 'signin' || !answer.confirmed) return this.declined()
    this.confirmed = true
    const credentialNote =
      credentials.length > 0 ? ((await this.connect(signal)) ?? '') : ''
    if (signal.aborted) return this.declined()
    return { status: 'confirmed', note: labSignInConfirmedNote(credentialNote) }
  }

  /**
   * The masked form. The credential note the model reads (names only), or
   * null when the user skipped or the deadline passed -- in which case the
   * model still reads why, in the declined note's place.
   */
  private async connect(signal: AbortSignal): Promise<string | null> {
    const { credentials, offerName } = this.deps
    const answer = await this.wait(
      { kind: 'credentials', offerName, credentials, error: null },
      signal,
    )
    if (answer.kind !== 'credentials') return null
    const values: Record<string, string> = {}
    for (const credential of credentials)
      values[credential.env] = answer.values[credential.env]!.trim()
    this.deps.held.hold(
      credentials.map((credential) => ({
        name: credential.env,
        value: values[credential.env]!,
      })),
    )
    const savedTo = await this.deps.save(values).catch(() => undefined)
    return [
      labCredentialSection(credentials, 'provided', offerName),
      sponsoredCredentialSavedLine(credentials.length, savedTo),
    ].join('\n')
  }

  /** Show `ask` until the user answers, the run stops, or the deadline. */
  private wait(
    ask: SponsoredAccountAsk,
    signal: AbortSignal,
  ): Promise<SponsoredAccountAnswer | { kind: 'timeout' }> {
    return new Promise((resolve) => {
      let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null
      const finish = (answer: SponsoredAccountAnswer | 'timeout') => {
        if (this.waiting !== waiting) return
        this.waiting = null
        if (timer) clearTimeout(timer)
        signal.removeEventListener('abort', aborted)
        this.deps.show(null)
        resolve(answer === 'timeout' ? { kind: 'timeout' } : answer)
      }
      const aborted = () => finish({ kind: 'skip' })
      const waiting: Waiting = { ask, answer: finish }
      if (signal.aborted || this.disposed) {
        resolve({ kind: 'skip' })
        return
      }
      this.waiting = waiting
      signal.addEventListener('abort', aborted, { once: true })
      timer = setTimeout(
        () => finish('timeout'),
        Math.max(0, this.deps.deadlineMs() - this.deps.now()),
      )
      timer.unref?.()
      this.deps.show(ask)
    })
  }

  private declined(): SponsoredAccountResult {
    return { status: 'declined', note: LAB_SIGNUP_DECLINED_NOTE }
  }
}

import { LAB_SIGNUP_TOOL_DESCRIPTION } from '@codebuff/common/ads/agentic-ad-lab-run'
import { getCustomToolDefinition } from '@codebuff/sdk'
import { z } from 'zod'

import { SPONSORED_SIGNUP_TOOL_NAME } from './sponsored-account-step'

import type { SponsoredAccountResult } from './sponsored-account-step'
import type { CustomToolDefinition } from '@codebuff/sdk'

/**
 * `request_sponsored_signup` for a CLI sponsored run, the tool Desktop and the
 * Lab register: it takes no arguments, so the link, the question and the
 * form are the host's (`sponsored-account-step.ts`). It ends the agent step
 * and resolves once the user answered, skipped, or the run stopped. The
 * result never carries a credential's value.
 */
export function sponsoredSignupTool(
  request: () => Promise<SponsoredAccountResult>,
): CustomToolDefinition {
  return getCustomToolDefinition({
    toolName: SPONSORED_SIGNUP_TOOL_NAME,
    inputSchema: z.object({}),
    description: LAB_SIGNUP_TOOL_DESCRIPTION,
    endsAgentStep: true,
    exampleInputs: [{}],
    execute: async () => [{ type: 'json', value: await request() }],
  })
}

async function mount(
  ask: SponsoredAccountAsk,
  reply: (answer: SponsoredAccountAnswer) => string | null = () => null,
) {
  const answers: SponsoredAccountAnswer[] = []

    width: 70,

  })
  const root = createRoot(setup.renderer)

    flushSync(() => root.unmount())
    setup.renderer.destroy()

    root.render(

        ask={ask}
        onAnswer={(answer) => {
          answers.push(answer)

        onInterrupt={() => interrupts.push(1)}
      />,
    )
  })
  const settle = async () => {
    await setup.renderOnce()
    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 20))
    await setup.renderOnce()
  }
  await settle()
  return {
    answers,
    interrupts,
    input: setup.mockInput,
    frame: () => setup.captureCharFrame(),
    async act(fn: () => unknown) {
      await fn()
      await settle()
    },
  }
}

- File `cli/src/utils/__tests__/sponsored-account-step.test.ts`: "a credential-only campaign"; "the key form is the question; a short key is refused and the form stays up"; "skip is a decline, and a decline is final"; "the deadline continues without the key"; "a stopped run stops waiting"; "an unsaved key says so, so the model tells the user"; "a tracked signup link"; "opens the link without the email, asks, and a Yes confirms then asks for the key"

/**
 * The CLI's account step for a sponsored run (`sponsored-account-step.ts`):
 * the sign-in question, the masked key form, and what the model is told.
 * Everything is injected; nothing opens a browser or writes a file.
 */
import { describe, expect, test } from 'bun:test'
import { inspect } from 'node:util'
import {
  LAB_SIGNUP_DECLINED_NOTE,
  LAB_SIGNUP_NO_STEP_NOTE,
} from '@codebuff/common/ads/agentic-ad-lab-run'
import {
  SponsoredAccountStep,
  SponsoredHeldCredentials,
} from '../sponsored-account-step'
import type {
  SponsoredAccountAsk,
  SponsoredAccountStepDeps,
} from '../sponsored-account-step'
const KEY = 'acme_live_0123456789abcdef'
const CREDENTIAL = {
  env: 'ACME_API_KEY',
  label: 'Acme API key',
  getUrl: 'https://acme.example/keys',
}
function harness(overrides: Partial<SponsoredAccountStepDeps> = {}) {
  const shown: Array<SponsoredAccountAsk | null> = []
  const opened: string[] = []
  const saved: Array<Readonly<Record<string, string>>> = []
  let now = 1_000
  const held = new SponsoredHeldCredentials()
  const step = new SponsoredAccountStep({
    offerName: 'Acme',
    signup: undefined,
    credentials: [CREDENTIAL],
    held,
    openUrl: async (url) => {
      opened.push(url)
      return true
    },
    show: (ask) => shown.push(ask),
    save: async (values) => {
      saved.push(values)
      return '.env.local'
    },
    deadlineMs: () => now + 60_000,
    now: () => now,
    ...overrides,
  })
  return {
    step,
    held,

```diff
  * exactly as Desktop's card replaces its banner. It used to be a block in the
  * transcript, reachable only by typing `/ads:accept-proposal`, and it scrolled

  *

  *    answers plus the standing controls;
  *  - the `/ads:*` commands still work, for a terminal with no mouse.

 /** The chord that opens the details, as the dock prints it. */

 /** Clip to the available columns without wrapping — a terminal has no ellipsis box. */
 function clip(text: string, width: number): string {
   if (width <= 1) return ''
   return text.length <= width
     ? text
     : `${text.slice(0, Math.max(0, width - 1))}…`
 }
 
 /** The in-place run's progress and verdict, as the dock needs it. */
 export type SponsoredDockRun = {
   phase: 'accepting' | 'queued' | 'running' | 'delivered' | 'failed'
   changedFiles: readonly string[]
   undone: boolean
   /** The skill step the harness is checking now, for a driven run. */
   checkingStep?: { number: number; total: number; title: string } | null
+  /** When the turn started on this machine; the pill counts from it. */
+  runningSince?: number | null
+  /** The run is waiting on the user's sign-in or key, below the card. */
+  needsYou?: boolean
+  /** A driven run's step results, once its checks ran. */
+  steps?: readonly SponsoredProposalStep[]
+}
+
+/** How many changed files the finished card names before "and N more". */
+export const SPONSORED_CHANGED_FILES_SHOWN = 5
+
+/** The finished card's file list: the first few, then how many more. */
+export function sponsoredChangedFilesLines(files: readonly string[]): string[] {
+  const shown = files.slice(0, SPONSORED_CHANGED_FILES_SHOWN)
+  const rest = files.length - shown.length
+  return [
+    ...shown.map((file) => `  ${file}`),
+    ...(rest > 0 ? [`  and ${rest} more`] : []),
+  ]
+}
+
+/** Re-renders every second while `active`, for a live elapsed time. */
+function useSecondTick(active: boolean): number {
+  const [now, setNow] = useState(() => Date.now())
+  useEffect(() => {
+    if (!active) return
+    setNow(Date.now())
+    const timer = setInterval(() => setNow(Date.now()), 1000)
+    return () => clearInterval(timer)
+  }, [active])
+  return now
 }

   step: { number: number; total: number; title: string } | null | undefined,
 ): string | null {
   return step
     ? `Checking step ${step.number}/${step.total}: ${step.title}`
     : null
 }

+import {
+  decodePasteBytes,
+  stripAnsiSequences,
+  TextAttributes,
+} from '@opentui/core'
+import { useAppContext, useKeyboard } from '@opentui/react'
+import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
+
+import { TerminalLink } from './terminal-link'
+import { useTheme } from '../hooks/use-theme'
+import { safeOpen } from '../utils/open-url'
+import {
+  SPONSORED_SIGNUP_NO,
+  SPONSORED_SIGNUP_YES,
+} from '../utils/sponsored-account-step'
+import { isPlainEnterKey } from '../utils/terminal-enter-detection'
+import { BORDER_CHARS } from '../utils/ui-constants'
+
+import type {
+  SponsoredAccountAnswer,
+  SponsoredAccountAsk,
+} from '../utils/sponsored-account-step'
+import type { KeyEvent, PasteEvent } from '@opentui/core'
+
+/** What the masked field shows instead of the key. */
+export function maskedSponsoredKey(value: string): string {
+  return value.length === 0 ? '' : '•'.repeat(Math.min(value.length, 32))
+}
+
+function preventDefault(key: KeyEvent): void {
+  if ('preventDefault' in key && typeof key.preventDefault === 'function')
+    key.preventDefault()
+}
+
+/** A printable key press, or null (control keys, modifiers). */
+function typed(key: KeyEvent): string | null {
+  if (!key.sequence || key.ctrl || key.meta || key.option) return null
+  return /^[\x21-\x7e]+$/.test(key.sequence) ? key.sequence : null
+}
+
+/**
+ * The account step a sponsored run is waiting on, in the composer's place
+ * (`sponsored-account-step.ts`): the sign-in question, or the masked key form.
+ *
+ * The key is typed or pasted into this component's own state and goes
+ * straight to the run's in-memory hold: never into the chat input, its
+ * history, the transcript or the model's context. It is drawn as dots.
+ */
+export const SponsoredAccountPrompt: React.FC<{
+  ask: SponsoredAccountAsk
+  onAnswer: (answer: SponsoredAccountAnswer) => string | null
+  /** Ctrl-C: stop the sponsored run, as it does everywhere else in chat. */
+  onInterrupt?: () => void
+}> = ({ ask, onAnswer, onInterrupt }) => {
+  const theme = useTheme()
+  const { keyHandler } = useAppContext()
+  const [choice, setChoice] = useState(0)
+  const [field, setField] = useState(0)
+  const [values, setValues] = useState<string[]>([])
+  const fields = ask.kind === 'credentials' ? ask.credentials : []
+
+  // A new question starts clean.
+  useEffect(() => {
+    setChoice(0)
+    setField(0)
+    setValues([])
+  }, [ask.kind])
+
+  const edit = useCallback(
+    (change: (value: string) => string) => {
+      setValues((current) => {
+        const next = [...current]
+        next[field] = change(current[field] ?? '').slice(0, 4096)
+        return next
+      })
+    },
+    [field],
+  )
+
+  const submit = useCallback(() => {
+    if (ask.kind === 'signin') {
+      onAnswer({ kind: 'signin', confirmed: choice === 0 })
+      return
+    }
+    if (field < fields.length - 1 && (values[field] ?? '').length > 0) {
+      setField(field + 1)
+      return
+    }
+    const answer: Record<string, string> = {}
+    fields.forEach((credential, index) => {
+      answer[credential.env] = values[index] ?? ''
+    })
+    onAnswer({ kind: 'credentials', values: answer })
+  }, [ask.kind, choice, field, fields, onAnswer, values])
+
+  // Pastes land in the focused key field, never in the chat input.
+  const pasteRef = useRef<(text: string) => void>(() => {})
+  pasteRef.current = (text) => {
+    if (ask.kind !== 'credentials') return
+    edit((value) => value + text.trim())
+  }
+  useEffect(() => {
+    if (!keyHandler) return
+    const onPaste = (event: PasteEvent) => {
+      pasteRef.current(stripAnsiSequences(decodePasteBytes(event.bytes)))
+    }
+    keyHandler.on('paste', onPaste)
+    return () => {
+      keyHandler.off('paste', onPaste)
+    }
+  }, [keyHandler])
+
+  useKeyboard(
+    useCallback(
+      (key: KeyEvent) => {
+        // Ctrl-C keeps its meaning: it stops the sponsored run. Esc skips
+        // only this step, and the run carries on without the account.
+        if (key.ctrl && key.name === 'c') {
+          preventDefault(key)
+          onAnswer({ kind: 'skip' })
+          onInterrupt?.()
+          return
+        }
+        if (key.name === 'escape') {
+          preventDefault(key)
+          onAnswer({ kind: 'skip' })
+          return
+        }
+        if (isPlainEnterKey(key)) {
+          preventDefault(key)
+          submit()
+          return
+        }
+        if (ask.kind === 'signin') {
+          if (key.name === 'up' || key.name === 'down' || key.name === 'tab') {
+            preventDefault(key)
+            setChoice((current) => (current === 0 ? 1 : 0))
+          } else if (key.name === 'y' && !key.ctrl && !key.meta) {
+            preventDefault(key)
+            onAnswer({ kind: 'signin', confirmed: true })
+          } else if (key.name === 'n' && !key.ctrl && !key.meta) {
+            preventDefault(key)
+            onAnswer({ kind: 'signin', confirmed: false })
+          }
+          return
+        }
+        if (key.name === 'backspace') {
+          preventDefault(key)
+          edit((value) => value.slice(0, -1))
+          return
+        }
+        if (key.name === 'tab' || key.name === 'up' || key.name === 'down') {
+          preventDefault(key)
+          if (fields.length > 1)
+            setField((current) =>
+              key.name === 'up' || key.shift
+                ? Math.max(0, current - 1)
+                : Math.min(fields.length - 1, current + 1),
+            )
+          return
+        }
+        const text = typed(key)
+        if (text !== null) {
+          preventDefault(key)
+          edit((value) => value + text)
+        }
+      },
+      [ask.kind, edit, fields.length, onAnswer, onInterrupt, submit],
+    ),
+  )
+
+  const title =
+    ask.kind === 'signin'
+      ? ' Sign in to continue '
+      : ` Connect ${ask.offerName} `
+
+  return (
+    <box
+      title={title}
+      titleAlignment="center"
+      style={{
+        width: '100%',
+        flexDirection: 'column',
+        borderStyle: 'single',
+        borderColor: theme.primary,
+        customBorderChars: BORDER_CHARS,
+        paddingLeft: 1,
+        paddingRight: 1,
+      }}
+    >
+      {ask.kind === 'signin' ? (
+        <>
+          <text style={{ fg: theme.foreground, wrapMode: 'word' }}>
+            {ask.opened
+              ? `To finish your free ${ask.offerName}, please sign in to the browser tab I just opened.`
+              : `To finish your free ${ask.offerName}, please sign in at this link:`}
+          </text>
+          <TerminalLink
+            text={ask.url}
+            lineWrap
+            onActivate={() => safeOpen(ask.url)}
+          />
+          {[SPONSORED_SIGNUP_YES, SPONSORED_SIGNUP_NO].map((label, index) => (
+            <text
+              key={label}
+              style={{
+                fg: index === choice ? theme.primary : theme.muted,
+              }}
+              attributes={index === choice ? TextAttributes.BOLD : undefined}
+            >
+              {`${index === choice ? '›' : ' '} ${label}`}
+            </text>
+          ))}
+          <text style={{ fg: theme.muted }}>
+            ↑↓ choose · Enter confirm · Esc no
+          </text>
+        </>
+      ) : (
+        <>
+          <text style={{ fg: theme.foreground, wrapMode: 'word' }}>
+            {`The sponsored task needs your ${ask.offerName} key. It is used for this task's commands, saved to your project's git-ignored env file, and never shown to the agent.`}
+          </text>
+          {fields.map((credential, index) => (
+            <box key={credential.env} style={{ flexDirection: 'column' }}>
+              <text
+                style={{ fg: index === field ? theme.primary : theme.muted }}
+                attributes={index === field ? TextAttributes.BOLD : undefined}
+              >
+                {`${credential.label} (${credential.env})`}
+              </text>
+              {credential.getUrl ? (
+                <TerminalLink
+                  text={credential.getUrl}
+                  lineWrap
+                  onActivate={() => safeOpen(credential.getUrl!)}
+                />
+              ) : null}
+              <text style={{ fg: theme.foreground }}>
+                {`${index === field ? '›' : ' '} ${maskedSponsoredKey(values[index] ?? '')}${index === field ? '▏' : ''}`}
+              </text>
+            </box>
+          ))}
+          {ask.error ? (
+            <text style={{ fg: theme.error, wrapMode: 'word' }}>
+              {ask.error}
+            </text>
+          ) : null}
+          <text style={{ fg: theme.muted }}>
+            Paste or type the key · Enter connect · Esc skip
+          </text>
+        </>
+      )}
+    </box>
+  )
+}
diff --git a/cli/src/utils/sponsored-account-step.ts b/cli/src/utils/sponsored-account-step.ts

+/**
+ * The account step of a CLI sponsored run: the sign-in and the API key a
+ * skill's user step needs (COD-665, COD-827 on Desktop).
+ *
+ * THE SAME SHAPE AS DESKTOP'S (`SponsoredSignupHandoff` in
+ * `freebuff-desktop/src/server/services/sponsored-signup.ts`), and the words
+ * the model reads are the shared ones the Lab already mirrors
+ * (`agentic-ad-lab-run.ts`, held to Desktop's text by
+ * `lab-sponsored-parity.test.ts`):
+ * - The turn is queued at once. The agent does every step that needs no
+ *   account, then calls `request_sponsored_signup` (no arguments: the link,
+ *   the question and the form are the host's, never the model's).
+ * - With a tracked signup link: the CLI opens it in the user's browser, prints
+ *   it, and asks whether they signed in. A Yes satisfies a skill's
+ *   `user_confirmed` check. Then any declared credentials are asked for.
+ * - Without one (a credential-only campaign): the masked key form IS the
+ *   question, and Skip is the decline.
+ * - A decline, a skip or the deadline is final for the run.
+ *
+ * A key is held in memory for THIS run only ({@link SponsoredHeldCredentials}),
+ * injected only as its declared variable into the run's terminal commands,
+ * redacted from everything the model reads, and saved for the user to the
+ * project's git-ignored env file (`sponsored-env-file.ts`), which the run
+ * itself can never read.
+ *
+ * Not ported: Desktop's Instant setup and its `{email}` prefill question. The
+ * `{email}` slot is removed from the link, so the email never reaches the
+ * advertiser.
+ */
+import {
+  LAB_SIGNUP_DECLINED_NOTE,
+  LAB_SIGNUP_NO_STEP_NOTE,
+  LAB_SIGNUP_STOPPED_NOTE,
+  labConnectedNote,
+  labCredentialSection,
+  labSignInConfirmedNote,
+} from '@codebuff/common/ads/agentic-ad-lab-run'
+import { signupUrlWithEmail } from '@codebuff/common/ads/sponsored-instant-setup'
+import {
+  redactSponsoredCredentialDeep,
+  redactSponsoredCredentialText,
+  sponsoredCredentialValueProblem,
+} from '@codebuff/common/ads/sponsored-run-credentials'
+import { inspect } from 'node:util'
+
+import type {
+  SponsoredRedactableSecret,
+  SponsoredRunCredential,
+} from '@codebuff/common/ads/sponsored-run-credentials'
+import type { SponsoredSignupStep } from '@codebuff/common/ads/sponsored-signup-step'
+
+export const SPONSORED_SIGNUP_TOOL_NAME = 'request_sponsored_signup'
+
+/**
+ * How long before the run's local cutoff the account step gives up, as
+ * Desktop's `SPONSORED_CONNECT_GRANT_MARGIN_MS`: the question has no timer of
+ * its own, so it waits while the grant can still fund the rest of the run.
+ */
+export const SPONSORED_ACCOUNT_STEP_MARGIN_MS = 5 * 60_000
+
+/** MIRRORED: Desktop's answers to the sign-in question. */
+export const SPONSORED_SIGNUP_YES = 'Yes, I’m signed in'
+export const SPONSORED_SIGNUP_NO = 'No, I don’t want to sign up'
+
+/** What the chat shows while the run waits on the user. Never a value. */
+export type SponsoredAccountAsk =
+  | {
+      kind: 'signin'
+      offerName: string
+      url: string
+      /** False when no browser could be opened here: the link is the way in. */
+      opened: boolean
+    }
+  | {
+      kind: 'credentials'
+      offerName: string
+      credentials: readonly SponsoredRunCredential[]
+      /** Why the last submission was refused, to show under the field. */
+      error: string | null
+    }
+
+export type SponsoredAccountAnswer =
+  | { kind: 'signin'; confirmed: boolean }
+  | { kind: 'credentials'; values: Readonly<Record<string, string>> }
+  | { kind: 'skip' }
+
+export type SponsoredAccountResult = {
+  status: 'confirmed' | 'declined' | 'unavailable'
+  note: string
+}
+
+/**
+ * A run's connected credentials. A class with a PRIVATE field, as Desktop's
+ * `SponsoredRunCredentialSet`: spread, `Object.entries`, `JSON.stringify` and
+ * `util.inspect` see nothing, so a stray log line cannot carry a value.
+ */
+export class SponsoredHeldCredentials {
+  #secrets: SponsoredRedactableSecret[] = []
+
+  hold(secrets: readonly SponsoredRedactableSecret[]): void {
+    this.#secrets = secrets.map((secret) => ({ ...secret }))
+  }
+
+  forget(): void {
+    this.#secrets = []
+  }
+
+  /** The declared names held. Names are not secret. */
+  get names(): string[] {
+    return this.#secrets.map((secret) => secret.name)
+  }
+
+  get size(): number {
+    return this.#secrets.length
+  }
+
+  /** What the sandbox broker injects, after its scrub. A fresh object. */
+  env(): Record<string, string> | undefined {
+    if (this.#secrets.length === 0) return undefined
+    const env: Record<string, string> = {}
+    for (const secret of this.#secrets) env[secret.name] = secret.value
+    return env
+  }
+
+  redact(text: string): string {
+    return this.#secrets.length === 0
+      ? text
+      : redactSponsoredCredentialText(text, this.#secrets)
+  }
+
+  redactDeep<T>(value: T): T {
+    return this.#secrets.length === 0
+      ? value
+      : redactSponsoredCredentialDeep(value, this.#secrets)
+  }
+
+  toJSON(): string {
+    return '[redacted]'
+  }
+
+  toString(): string {
+    return '[redacted]'
+  }
+
+  [inspect.custom](): string {
+    return `SponsoredHeldCredentials(${this.names.join(', ') || 'empty'}) [redacted]`
+  }
+}
+
+/** User copy for a refused key. Never names a value. */
+export function sponsoredCredentialRefusalCopy(
+  credential: SponsoredRunCredential,
+  problem: string,
+): string {
+  if (problem === 'empty') return `Enter your ${credential.label}, or skip.`
+  if (problem === 'too_short')
+    return `That ${credential.label} looks too short. Paste the whole key.`
+  if (problem === 'too_long')
+    return `That ${credential.label} is too long. Paste the key on its own.`
+  return `That ${credential.label} has spaces or line breaks in it. Paste the key on its own.`
+}
+
+/**
+ * MIRRORED from Desktop's `sponsoredCredentialSection` `savedTo` lines: where
+ * the key went for the app to read after the run.
+ */
+export function sponsoredCredentialSavedLine(
+  count: number,
+  savedTo: string | undefined,
+): string {
+  return savedTo
+    ? `[freebuff product prompt text omitted]${count === 1 ? 'it' : 'them'}[freebuff product prompt text omitted]${savedTo}[freebuff product prompt text omitted]${count === 1 ? '' : 's'}[freebuff product prompt text omitted]${count === 1 ? 'it' : 'them'}[freebuff product prompt text omitted]${savedTo}[freebuff product prompt text omitted]`
+    : '- Freebuff did NOT save the value to an env file in this project, and this run cannot write one. In your final message, tell the user to add it to their local env file themselves, naming the variable.'
+}
+
+export type SponsoredAccountStepDeps = {
+  offerName: string
+  /** The tracked signup link (`sponsoredSignupStep`), or none. */
+  signup: SponsoredSignupStep | undefined
+  /** What the procedure declares (`declaredRunCredentials`). */
+  credentials: readonly SponsoredRunCredential[]
+  held: SponsoredHeldCredentials
+  /** Opens a link in the user's browser; false when none could be opened. */
+  openUrl: (url: string) => Promise<boolean>
+  /** Shows (or, with null, clears) what the chat asks the user. */
+  show: (ask: SponsoredAccountAsk | null) => void
+  /** Saves accepted keys to the project's env file; the file name, if saved. */
+  save: (
+    values: Readonly<Record<string, string>>,
+  ) => Promise<string | undefined>
+  /** When the step stops waiting and continues without the user. */
+  deadlineMs: () => number
+  now: () => number
+}
+
+type Waiting = {
+  ask: SponsoredAccountAsk
+  answer: (answer: SponsoredAccountAnswer | 'timeout') => void
+}
+
+/**
+ * One run's account step. `request` is what the tool calls; `answer` is what
+ * the chat calls when the user responds.
+ */
+export class SponsoredAccountStep {
+  private result: SponsoredAccountResult | null = null
+  private pending: Promise<SponsoredAccountResult> | null = null
+  private waiting: Waiting | null = null
+  private confirmed = false
+  private disposed = false
+
+  constructor(private readonly deps: SponsoredAccountStepDeps) {}
+
+  /** The user said Yes to the sign-in: a skill's `user_confirmed` passes. */
+  get signupConfirmed(): boolean {
+    return this.confirmed
+  }
+
+  /** Whether the run is waiting on the user right now. */
+  get asking(): SponsoredAccountAsk | null {
+    return this.waiting?.ask ?? null
+  }
+
+  /** The run ended: anything waiting continues as declined. */
+  dispose(): void {
+    this.disposed = true
+    this.waiting?.answer({ kind: 'skip' })
+  }
+
+  request(signal: AbortSignal): Promise<SponsoredAccountResult> {
+    if (signal.aborted || this.disposed)
+      return Promise.resolve({
+        status: 'unavailable',
+        note: LAB_SIGNUP_STOPPED_NOTE,
+      })
+    if (this.result) return Promise.resolve(this.result)
+    if (this.pending) return this.pending
+    this.pending = this.run(signal)
+      .then((result) => {
+        if (result.status !== 'unavailable') this.result = result
+        return result
+      })
+      .finally(() => {
+        this.pending = null
+      })
+    return this.pending
+  }
+
+  /**
+   * The user's response. A refused key leaves the form up with the reason and
+   * returns it; everything else resolves the step and returns null.
+   */
+  answer(answer: SponsoredAccountAnswer): string | null {
+    const waiting = this.waiting
+    if (!waiting) return null
+    if (answer.kind === 'credentials' && waiting.ask.kind === 'credentials') {
+      for (const credential of waiting.ask.credentials) {
+        const value = answer.values[credential.env]?.trim() ?? ''
+        const problem = sponsoredCredentialValueProblem(value)
+        if (problem) {
+          const error = sponsoredCredentialRefusalCopy(credential, problem)
+          waiting.ask = { ...waiting.ask, error }
+          this.deps.show(waiting.ask)
+          return error
+        }
+      }
+    }
+    waiting.answer(answer)
+    return null
+  }
+
+  private async run(signal: AbortSignal): Promise<SponsoredAccountResult> {
+    const { signup, credentials } = this.deps
+    if (!signup) {
+      if (credentials.length === 0)
+        return { status: 'unavailable', note: LAB_SIGNUP_NO_STEP_NOTE }
+      const connected = await this.connect(signal)
+      if (!connected) return this.declined()
+      return { status: 'confirmed', note: labConnectedNote(connected) }
+    }
+    // The `{email}` slot, if any, is removed: no prefill without consent.
+    const url = signup.url ? signupUrlWithEmail(signup.url, null) : null
+    if (!url) {
+      return {
+        status: 'unavailable',
+        note: 'Tracked signup is unavailable. Do not open an untracked link. Report the remaining account-dependent steps as blocked.',
+      }
+    }
+    const opened = await this.deps.openUrl(url).catch(() => false)
+    const answer = await this.wait(
+      { kind: 'signin', offerName: this.deps.offerName, url, opened },
+      signal,
+    )
+    if (answer.kind !== 'signin' || !answer.confirmed) return this.declined()
+    this.confirmed = true
+    const credentialNote =
+      credentials.length > 0 ? ((await this.connect(signal)) ?? '') : ''
+    if (signal.aborted) return this.declined()
+    return { status: 'confirmed', note: labSignInConfirmedNote(credentialNote) }
+  }
+
+  /**
+   * The masked form. The credential note the model reads (names only), or
+   * null when the user skipped or the deadline passed -- in which case the
+   * model still reads why, in the declined note's place.
+   */
+  private async connect(signal: AbortSignal): Promise<string | null> {
+    const { credentials, offerName } = this.deps
+    const answer = await this.wait(
+      { kind: 'credentials', offerName, credentials, error: null },
+      signal,
+    )
+    if (answer.kind !== 'credentials') return null
+    const values: Record<string, string> = {}
+    for (const credential of credentials)
+      values[credential.env] = answer.values[credential.env]!.trim()
+    this.deps.held.hold(
+      credentials.map((credential) => ({
+        name: credential.env,
+        value: values[credential.env]!,
+      })),
+    )
+    const savedTo = await this.deps.save(values).catch(() => undefined)
+    return [
+      labCredentialSection(credentials, 'provided', offerName),
+      sponsoredCredentialSavedLine(credentials.length, savedTo),
+    ].join('\n')
+  }
+
+  /** Show `ask` until the user answers, the run stops, or the deadline. */
+  private wait(
+    ask: SponsoredAccountAsk,
+    signal: AbortSignal,
+  ): Promise<SponsoredAccountAnswer | { kind: 'timeout' }> {
+    return new Promise((resolve) => {
+      let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null
+      const finish = (answer: SponsoredAccountAnswer | 'timeout') => {
+        if (this.waiting !== waiting) return
+        this.waiting = null
+        if (timer) clearTimeout(timer)
+        signal.removeEventListener('abort', aborted)
+        this.deps.show(null)
+        resolve(answer === 'timeout' ? { kind: 'timeout' } : answer)
+      }
+      const aborted = () => finish({ kind: 'skip' })
+      const waiting: Waiting = { ask, answer: finish }
+      if (signal.aborted || this.disposed) {
+        resolve({ kind: 'skip' })
+        return
+      }
+      this.waiting = waiting
+      signal.addEventListener('abort', aborted, { once: true })
+      timer = setTimeout(
+        () => finish('timeout'),
+        Math.max(0, this.deps.deadlineMs() - this.deps.now()),
+      )
+      timer.unref?.()
+      this.deps.show(ask)
+    })
+  }
+
+  private declined(): SponsoredAccountResult {
+    return { status: 'declined', note: LAB_SIGNUP_DECLINED_NOTE }
+  }
+}

   runtimeInputs: SponsoredProcedureRuntimeInputs = {},

 /**
  * The definition a sponsored turn runs as.
  *
  * `noAskUser` is passed as well as the toolName narrowing below, and both are
  * deliberate: the flag removes the two human tools AND the paragraph of the
  * appendix that tells the model to use them, so the run is not being told to do
  * something its toolset refuses. The narrowing is what makes it true.
  */
 export function sponsoredAgentDefinition(options: {
   agentId: string
   model?: string
   isFreebuff: boolean
   grant?: ReadonlySet<SponsoredCapability>
   /** Offer `report_friction` (registered by the caller as a custom tool). */
   reportFriction?: boolean
+  /** Offer `request_sponsored_signup`: the run has an account step. */
+  requestSignup?: boolean
 }): AgentDefinition {
   const {
     agentId,
     model,
     isFreebuff,
     grant = SPONSORED_LOCAL_V1_GRANT,
     reportFriction = false,
+    requestSignup = false,
   } = options
   const root = createBase3CliRoot({
     ...(model ? { model } : {}),
     isFreebuff,
     noAskUser: true,
   })
   return {
     ...root,
     id: agentId,
     // A NARROWING of what the root already offers, never a replacement: a
     // sponsored run has to be a subset of an ordinary one, so a tool the root
     // does not have cannot appear here by way of the policy granting its
     // capability.
     // `report_friction` is ours, not the root's: a custom tool that touches
     // nothing of the user's, so it is added beside the narrowing.
     toolNames: [
       ...sponsoredLocalToolNames(root.toolNames ?? [], grant),
       ...(reportFriction ? [SPONSORED_FRICTION_TOOL_NAME] : []),
+      ...(requestSignup ? [SPONSORED_SIGNUP_TOOL_NAME] : []),
     ],
     systemPrompt: `${root.systemPrompt}\n\n# Sponsored task\n\n${CLI_SPONSORED_GUIDANCE}${
       reportFriction ? `\n${SPONSORED_FRICTION_BULLET}` : ''
-    }`,
+    }${requestSignup ? `\n${SPONSORED_SIGNUP_BULLET}` : ''}`,
     // Nothing is committed by an in-place run; kept so a commit that somehow
     // happened anyway could not be attributed to the user.
     suppressCommitAttribution: true,
   } as AgentDefinition
 }

 /**
  * `overrideTools` for an in-place sponsored turn.
  *
  * Exported so the clamps can be asserted directly rather than only through a
  * live run: every entry here is a boundary, and a boundary that is only
  * exercised end-to-end is a boundary nobody tests.
  */
 export function sponsoredOverrideTools(
   context: SponsoredToolContext,
 ): SponsoredOverrideTools {
   const workspaceRoot = context.workspaceRoot
   const processBroker = createSponsoredCodeSearchBroker({
     workspaceRoot,
     runtimeDir: context.runtimeDir,
     // The workspace's own `.git` is inside the write root and must stay
     // read-only to the run: no commit, no ref, and above all no `hooks/` or
     // `config`, which an unsandboxed `git -C` would later execute as the user.
     readOnlyGitDir: true,
     // The user's own Node/npm/bun under their home folder, READ-ONLY, or the
     // private HOME hides it and the run finds no Node (COD-825).
     additionalReadRoots: sponsoredToolchainReadRoots(
       getSystemProcessEnv(),
       homedir(),
       (skip) =>
         logger.info(skip, '[sponsored-run] toolchain folder not granted'),
     ).roots,
+    // The keys the user connected (`sponsored-account-step.ts`), as exactly
+    // their declared variables. Every result is redacted on the way back
+    // (`redactSponsoredOverrideTools`).
+    ...(context.credentialEnv
+      ? {
+          get credentialEnv() {
+            return context.credentialEnv?.()
+          },
+        }
+      : {}),
   })
   // No broker: the file layer pins directories itself (COD-642).
   const rootedFs = createSponsoredRootedFileSystem({ workspaceRoot })
   const recorded = async <T>(
     requestedPath: string,
     write: () => Promise<T>,
   ) => {
     try {
       return await context.recorder.around(requestedPath, write)
     } catch (error) {
       // A write the undo could not reverse is refused, not performed.
       if (error instanceof SponsoredReceiptRefusal) {
         return refusal(error.message) as unknown as T
       }
       throw error
     }
   }
   return {
     read_files: async (input: {
       filePaths: string[]
       fileWindows?: Record<string, FileReadWindow[]>
     }) => {
       const out: Record<string, string | null> = {}
       const allowed: string[] = []
       for (const filePath of input.filePaths ?? []) {
@@ -2190,50 +2314,112 @@ export function withSponsoredModelEventTap<

 /**
  * `tools`, each of which first calls `first` -- the turn's first tool call is
  * the CLI's proof that its compute started (`computeStarted`). `first` must
  * not throw and is not awaited: the report it starts must never slow a tool.
  */
 function beforeFirstToolCall<T extends object>(tools: T, first: () => void): T {
   const wrapped: Record<string, unknown> = {}
   for (const [name, handler] of Object.entries(tools)) {
     wrapped[name] =
       typeof handler === 'function'
         ? (...args: unknown[]) => {
             first()
             return (handler as (...args: unknown[]) => unknown)(...args)
           }
         : handler
   }
   return wrapped as T
 }
 
+/**
+ * Every tool result redacted of the keys the user connected for this run,
+ * as Desktop's `redactSponsoredToolResults`: the broker injects a value into
+ * a command's environment, and whatever the command prints comes back here
+ * before the model (or a step check's fix-up prompt) reads it. A thrown
+ * message is redacted too. Read at each call: a key may arrive mid-turn.
+ */
+export function redactSponsoredOverrideTools<T extends object>(
+  tools: T,
+  credentials: SponsoredHeldCredentials,
+): T {
+  const wrapped: Record<string, unknown> = {}
+  for (const [name, handler] of Object.entries(tools)) {
+    wrapped[name] =
+      typeof handler === 'function'
+        ? async (...args: unknown[]) => {
+            try {
+              return credentials.redactDeep(
+                await (handler as (...args: unknown[]) => unknown)(...args),
+              )
+            } catch (error) {
+              if (credentials.size === 0) throw error
+              throw new Error(
+                credentials.redact(
+                  error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
+                ),
+              )
+            }
+          }
+        : handler
+  }
+  return wrapped as T
+}
+
+/**
+ * Save connected keys where the user's app reads them: the project's local
+ * env file, only when the project's own git rules ignore it, staged inside
+ * git's own directory (`sponsored-env-file.ts`, shared with Desktop). Never
+ * throws and never logs a value.
+ */
+export async function saveSponsoredCredentialsForProject(
+  workspace: string,
+  values: Readonly<Record<string, string>>,
+  git: GitRunner = bunGitRunner,
+): Promise<string | undefined> {
+  const outcome = await saveSponsoredCredentialsToEnvFile({
+    workspace,
+    values,
+    isIgnored: async (root, relative) =>
+      (await git(['check-ignore', '-q', '--', relative], root)).exitCode === 0,
+    stagingDir: async (root) => {
+      const dir = await git(['rev-parse', '--absolute-git-dir'], root)
+      return dir.exitCode === 0 ? dir.stdout.trim() || null : null
+    },
+  }).catch(() => ({ saved: false as const, reason: 'io' as const }))
+  logger.info(
+    outcome.saved ? { file: outcome.file } : { reason: outcome.reason },
+    '[sponsored-run] connected credentials env file',
+  )
+  return outcome.saved ? outcome.file : undefined
+}
+
 function fileToolPath(input: unknown): string | null {
   if (!input || typeof input !== 'object') return null
   const value = input as { path?: unknown; operation?: { path?: unknown } }
   if (typeof value.path === 'string') return value.path
   return typeof value.operation?.path === 'string' ? value.operation.path : null
 }

diff --git a/common/src/ads/sponsored-env-file.ts b/common/src/ads/sponsored-env-file.ts
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..34980c121
--- /dev/null
+++ b/common/src/ads/sponsored-env-file.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,202 @@
+/**
+ * Saving a connected credential where the user's app reads it.
+ *
+ * A sponsored run may not read or write `.env` files (the sandbox and the
+ * write policy both refuse it), so a key the user connects reached the run's
+ * terminal commands and nothing else: the feature the run built had no key
+ * once the run ended. The ORCHESTRATOR writes it instead, as the user, into
+ * the project's local env file. The model never sees the value, and the file
+ * stays unreadable to the run.
+ *
+ * Refused, and reported as such, unless every one of these holds:
+ * - the file is ignored by the project's own git rules, so a key can never
+ *   be committed (a folder that is not a git repository is refused);
+ * - the path is a plain file or absent, never a symlink out of the project,
+ *   and the write replaces the directory entry instead of opening it, so a
+ *   link swapped in after the check is replaced, not followed; a file that
+ *   appears where none was is read and kept, never overwritten;
+ * - the staging copy is as uncommittable as the target: in git's own
+ *   directory, or beside the target only when git ignores that name too;
+ * - the value can be written as one dotenv line that reads back unchanged.
+ */
+
+import { randomBytes } from 'node:crypto'
+import { constants } from 'node:fs'
+import {
+  chmod,
+  link,
+  lstat,
+  open,
+  readFile,
+  rename,
+  rm,
+  writeFile,
+} from 'node:fs/promises'
+import { join } from 'node:path'
+
+export type SponsoredEnvFileOutcome =
+  | { saved: true; file: string }
+  | {
+      saved: false
+      reason: 'no_workspace' | 'not_ignored' | 'not_a_file' | 'value' | 'io'
+    }
+
+const BARE_VALUE = /^[A-Za-z0-9_\-./+=:@~%,]+$/
+
+/**
+ * `NAME=value` as one dotenv line. Single quotes are literal in dotenv and in
+ * the `dotenv-expand` Next.js runs, so `$` and `#` survive; a value holding a
+ * single quote has no safe spelling and is refused.
+ */
+export function sponsoredEnvLine(name: string, value: string): string | null {
+  if (!value || /[\s\u0000-\u001f\u007f]/.test(value)) return null
+  if (BARE_VALUE.test(value)) return `${name}=${value}`
+  if (value.includes("'")) return null
+  return `${name}='${value}'`
+}
+
+/** Replaces each name's existing line, or appends it; every other line is kept. */
+export function upsertSponsoredEnvLines(
+  text: string,
+  lines: Readonly<Record<string, string>>,
+): string {
+  const eol = text.includes('\r\n') ? '\r\n' : '\n'
+  const rows = text === '' ? [] : text.replace(/\r?\n$/, '').split(/\r?\n/)
+  for (const [name, line] of Object.entries(lines)) {
+    const own = new RegExp(`^\\s*(?:export\\s+)?${name}\\s*=`)
+    const at = rows.findIndex((row) => own.test(row))
+    if (at === -1) rows.push(line)
+    else {
+      // One line per name: a duplicate further down would win in dotenv.
+      for (let i = rows.length - 1; i > at; i--)
+        if (own.test(rows[i]!)) rows.splice(i, 1)
+      rows[at] = line
+    }
+  }
+  return rows.join(eol) + eol
+}
+
+/**
+ * The env file this project's server reads for local secrets: an existing
+ * `.env.local`, else `.env.local` for Next.js and Vite, else `.env`.
+ */
+export async function sponsoredEnvFileFor(
+  workspace: string,
+): Promise<'.env.local' | '.env'> {
+  if (await entry(join(workspace, '.env.local'))) return '.env.local'
+  try {
+    const pkg = JSON.parse(
+      await readFile(join(workspace, 'package.json'), 'utf8'),
+    ) as Record<string, Record<string, unknown> | undefined>
+    const deps = { ...pkg.dependencies, ...pkg.devDependencies }
+    if ('next' in deps || 'vite' in deps) return '.env.local'
+  } catch {
+    // No readable package.json: a plain Node project.
+  }
+  return '.env'
+}
+
+/** A regular file's text and mode, null when absent, refused when anything else. */
+async function plainFile(
+  path: string,
+): Promise<{ text: string; mode: number } | null | 'not_a_file'> {
+  // Windows has no O_NOFOLLOW, so a link is refused by name first there too.
+  const seen = await entry(path)
+  if (seen && !seen.isFile()) return 'not_a_file'
+  let handle
+  try {
+    handle = await open(path, constants.O_RDONLY | (constants.O_NOFOLLOW ?? 0))
+  } catch (error) {
+    return (error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code === 'ENOENT'
+      ? null
+      : 'not_a_file'
+  }
+  try {
+    const stat = await handle.stat()
+    if (!stat.isFile()) return 'not_a_file'
+    return { text: await handle.readFile('utf8'), mode: stat.mode }
+  } finally {
+    await handle.close()
+  }
+}
+
+/** lstat, not stat: a symlink is seen as itself, wherever it points. */
+const entry = (path: string) => lstat(path).catch(() => null)
+
+export async function saveSponsoredCredentialsToEnvFile(input: {
+  workspace: string | undefined
+  /** Declared names to validated values. */
+  values: Readonly<Record<string, string>>
+  /** The project's own git rules: true only when git says the path is ignored. */
+  isIgnored: (workspace: string, relative: string) => Promise<boolean>
+  /** Git's own directory for this checkout, where nothing can be staged for commit. */
+  stagingDir?: (workspace: string) => Promise<string | null>
+}): Promise<SponsoredEnvFileOutcome> {
+  const { workspace, values } = input
+  if (!workspace || !(await entry(workspace))?.isDirectory())
+    return { saved: false, reason: 'no_workspace' }
+  const lines: Record<string, string> = {}
+  for (const [name, value] of Object.entries(values)) {
+    const line = sponsoredEnvLine(name, value)
+    if (!line) return { saved: false, reason: 'value' }
+    lines[name] = line
+  }
+  if (Object.keys(lines).length === 0) return { saved: false, reason: 'value' }
+  try {
+    const file = await sponsoredEnvFileFor(workspace)
+    const path = join(workspace, file)
+    // Twice at most: a file another process creates while this one works is
+    // read and kept, never overwritten.
+    for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 2; attempt++) {
+      // Read through one no-follow handle, before anything else is awaited: a
+      // symlink or a directory is refused, and what is read is the file checked.
+      const existing = await plainFile(path)
+      if (existing === 'not_a_file')
+        return { saved: false, reason: 'not_a_file' }
+      if (!(await input.isIgnored(workspace, file)))
+        return { saved: false, reason: 'not_ignored' }
+      // The staging file holds the key in plain text, so it must be as
+      // uncommittable as the target: inside git's own directory, or beside
+      // the target only when git ignores that name too.
+      const name = `${file}.${randomBytes(6).toString('hex')}.tmp`
+      const gitDir = await input.stagingDir?.(workspace).catch(() => null)
+      const staged =
+        gitDir && (await entry(gitDir))?.isDirectory()
+          ? join(gitDir, `freebuff-${name}`)
+          : (await input.isIgnored(workspace, name))
+            ? join(workspace, name)
+            : null
+      if (!staged) return { saved: false, reason: 'not_ignored' }
+      const mode = existing ? existing.mode & 0o777 : 0o600
+      try {
+        await writeFile(
+          staged,
+          upsertSponsoredEnvLines(existing?.text ?? '', lines),
+          { flag: 'wx', mode },
+        )
+        // The create mode passes through the umask; this one does not.
+        await chmod(staged, mode)
+        if (existing) {
+          // A rename replaces the directory entry: a symlink swapped in since
+          // the check is replaced, not followed, and a hard link elsewhere
+          // keeps the old content.
+          await rename(staged, path)
+          return { saved: true, file }
+        }
+        // Nothing was there: publish without replacing. A link fails when a
+        // file has appeared since the read, and the next attempt reads it.
+        try {
+          await link(staged, path)
+          return { saved: true, file }
+        } catch (error) {
+          if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code !== 'EEXIST') throw error
+        }
+      } finally {
+        await rm(staged, { force: true }).catch(() => {})
+      }
+    }
+    return { saved: false, reason: 'io' }
+  } catch {
+    return { saved: false, reason: 'io' }
+  }
+}

+import {
+  resolveSponsoredBfcidTemplate,
+  type SponsoredProcedureRuntimeInputs,
+} from '@codebuff/common/ads/sponsored-procedure-inputs'
+import { declaredRunCredentials } from '@codebuff/common/ads/sponsored-run-credentials'
+
+import type { SponsoredSkill } from '@codebuff/common/ads/sponsored-skill'
+
+export type SponsoredSignupStep = {
+  /** Null when the reviewed link cannot be resolved safely for this Accept. */
+  url: string | null
+  /** The account tab opened by Desktop, never supplied by the advertiser. */
+  tabId?: string
+}
+
+const MAX_SIGNUP_URL_CHARS = 2048
+
+/**
+ * Manual review makes the FIRST explicitly tracked link the account entry
+ * point. When the procedure has none but still needs an account (it declares
+ * a credential, or its skill has a user step), the Accept's own
+ * `advertiserLink` is the entry point: the reviewed campaign landing URL
+ * carrying the signed click id, so the account is still attributed. Without
+ * a usable one, nothing is opened, as before.
+ */
+export function sponsoredSignupStep(
+  procedure: string,
+  inputs: SponsoredProcedureRuntimeInputs,
+  skill?: SponsoredSkill | null,
+): SponsoredSignupStep | undefined {
+  for (const match of procedure.matchAll(/https:\/\/[^\s<>"'`]+/gi)) {
+    let raw = match[0].replace(/[.,;!?]+$/, '')
+    for (const [open, close] of [
+      ['(', ')'],
+      ['[', ']'],
+    ] as const) {
+      while (
+        raw.endsWith(close) &&
+        raw.split(close).length > raw.split(open).length
+      )
+        raw = raw.slice(0, -1).replace(/[.,;!?]+$/, '')
+    }
+    let url: URL
+    try {
+      url = new URL(raw)
+    } catch {
+      continue
+    }
+    const ids = url.searchParams.getAll('bfcid')
+    if (!ids.some((value) => /^\{bfcid\}$/i.test(value))) continue
+    if (
+      ids.length !== 1 ||
+      url.username ||
+      url.password ||
+      raw.length > MAX_SIGNUP_URL_CHARS
+    )
+      return { url: null }
+    return { url: resolveSponsoredBfcidTemplate(raw, inputs) }
+  }
+  const needsAccount =
+    declaredRunCredentials(procedure).length > 0 ||
+    Boolean(skill?.steps.some((step) => step.kind === 'user'))
+  const landing = needsAccount ? trackedAdvertiserLink(inputs) : null
+  return landing ? { url: landing } : undefined
+}
+
+/**
+ * The Accept's advertiser link when it is safe to open as the account entry
+ * point: absolute https, no credentials, within the size cap, and carrying
+ * exactly one SIGNED click id (never the `{bfcid}` slot), so opening it is
+ * attributed. Null otherwise.
+ */
+function trackedAdvertiserLink(
+  inputs: SponsoredProcedureRuntimeInputs,
+): string | null {
+  const raw = inputs.advertiserLink?.trim()
+  if (!raw || raw.length > MAX_SIGNUP_URL_CHARS) return null
+  let url: URL
+  try {
+    url = new URL(raw)
+  } catch {
+    return null
+  }
+  if (url.protocol !== 'https:' || url.username || url.password) return null
+  const ids = url.searchParams.getAll('bfcid')
+  return ids.length === 1 && ids[0] && !/[{}]/.test(ids[0]) ? url.href : null
+}

```
